SELECT [t].[Year], [t].[Month], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(0 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Actual], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(1 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Forecast]
FROM (
    SELECT [e].[IsForecasted], [e].[TotalAmount], DATEPART(year, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Year], DATEPART(month, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Month]
    FROM [Expenditures] AS [e]
    WHERE [e].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]
ORDER BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]

    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(0 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Actual], COALESCE(SUM(CASE

END), 0.0) AS [Forecast]

    FROM [Expenditures] AS [e]

) AS [t]

SELECT [t].[Year], [t].[Month], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(0 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Actual], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(1 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Forecast]
FROM (
    SELECT [e].[IsForecasted], [e].[TotalAmount], DATEPART(year, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Year], DATEPART(month, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Month]
    FROM [Expenditures] AS [e]
    WHERE [e].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t]

SELECT [t].[Year], [t].[Month], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(0 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Actual], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(1 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Forecast]
FROM (
    SELECT [e].[IsForecasted], [e].[TotalAmount], DATEPART(year, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Year], DATEPART(month, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Month]
    FROM [Expenditures] AS [e]
    WHERE [e].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]
ORDER BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]

SELECT [t].[Year], [t].[Month], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(0 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Actual], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(1 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Forecast]
FROM (
    SELECT [e].[IsForecasted], [e].[TotalAmount], DATEPART(year, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Year], DATEPART(month, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Month]
    FROM [Expenditures] AS [e]
    WHERE [e].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]
ORDER BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]

    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(0 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]

SELECT [t].[Year], [t].[Month], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(0 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Actual], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(1 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Forecast]
FROM (
    SELECT [e].[IsForecasted], [e].[TotalAmount], DATEPART(year, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Year], DATEPART(month, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Month]
    FROM [Expenditures] AS [e]
    WHERE [e].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]
ORDER BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]

SELECT [t].[Year], [t].[Month], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(0 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Actual], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(1 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Forecast]
FROM (
    SELECT [e].[IsForecasted], [e].[TotalAmount], DATEPART(year, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Year], DATEPART(month, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Month]
    FROM [Expenditures] AS [e]
    WHERE [e].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]
ORDER BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]

SELECT [t].[Year], [t].[Month], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(0 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Actual], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(1 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Forecast]
FROM (
    SELECT [e].[IsForecasted], [e].[TotalAmount], DATEPART(year, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Year], DATEPART(month, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Month]
    FROM [Expenditures] AS [e]
    WHERE [e].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]
ORDER BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]

SELECT [t].[Year], [t].[Month], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(0 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Actual], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(1 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Forecast]
FROM (
    SELECT [e].[IsForecasted], [e].[TotalAmount], DATEPART(year, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Year], DATEPART(month, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Month]
    FROM [Expenditures] AS [e]
    WHERE [e].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]
ORDER BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]
2026-08-07 08:05:24.944 +05:30 [INF] Executed DbCommand (5ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']

SELECT [t].[Year], [t].[Month], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(0 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Actual], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(1 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Forecast]
FROM (
    SELECT [e].[IsForecasted], [e].[TotalAmount], DATEPART(year, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Year], DATEPART(month, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Month]
    FROM [Expenditures] AS [e]
    WHERE [e].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]

    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(0 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]

SELECT [t].[Year], [t].[Month], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(0 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Actual], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(1 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Forecast]
FROM (
    SELECT [e].[IsForecasted], [e].[TotalAmount], DATEPART(year, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Year], DATEPART(month, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Month]
    FROM [Expenditures] AS [e]
    WHERE [e].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]
ORDER BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]

SELECT TOP(@__p_0) [t].[CompanyName] AS [VendorName], COALESCE(SUM([e].[TotalAmount]), 0.0) AS [Amount]
FROM [Expenditures] AS [e]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [v].[Id], [v].[CompanyName]
    FROM [Vendors] AS [v]
    WHERE [v].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t] ON [e].[VendorId] = [t].[Id]

SELECT [t].[Year], [t].[Month], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(0 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Actual], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(1 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Forecast]
FROM (
    SELECT [e].[IsForecasted], [e].[TotalAmount], DATEPART(year, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Year], DATEPART(month, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Month]
    FROM [Expenditures] AS [e]
    WHERE [e].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]
ORDER BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]

    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(0 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]

SELECT [t].[Year], [t].[Month], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(0 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Actual], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(1 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Forecast]
FROM (
    SELECT [e].[IsForecasted], [e].[TotalAmount], DATEPART(year, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Year], DATEPART(month, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Month]
    FROM [Expenditures] AS [e]
    WHERE [e].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]
ORDER BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]

SELECT [t].[Year], [t].[Month], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(0 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Actual], COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN [t].[IsForecasted] = CAST(1 AS bit) THEN [t].[TotalAmount]
END), 0.0) AS [Forecast]
FROM (
    SELECT [e].[IsForecasted], [e].[TotalAmount], DATEPART(year, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Year], DATEPART(month, [e].[ExpenseDate]) AS [Month]
    FROM [Expenditures] AS [e]
    WHERE [e].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]
ORDER BY [t].[Year], [t].[Month]
